**Known facts that shape the next waves:**
- Nothing but a hitch moves a cage ("Dịch chuyển" rejects blocks, a piston stops at the wall, a moving platform does not carry blocks). A cage set on a "Bệ di chuyển" stays behind when the bed leaves; tested recipe = one hitch on the bed per cage slot.
- A moving block or body is not stopped by fixed walls (engine rule); only a HELD body is.
- Tick cost of the grid sample headless: mean 6.1–8.5 ms, p99 10.1–15.6 ms (noisy machine) against a 16.7 ms budget, before drawing. 154 resting cartons miss the budget; 100 pass without robots.
- Suite time grew to 10–48 min depending on load (103 wire-cut runs of the grid sample are in the default suite).

## 1. Goal
An ORIGINAL browser node-wiring game builder for a Vietnamese non-coder user. You drag nodes ("mảnh ghép"), wire ports and press Chơi to play a 2D or 3D game.
- Claude writes all the code; the user only playtests and decides.
- It is NOT a clone of Game Builder Garage. Never read `D:\gbg\` and never mirror its nodes, UI, lessons or text. Generic genre mechanics are fine.

## 1b. 2026-11-01 afternoon — "Xưởng Bot giải đố" puzzle game (in main)
- **Open:** http://localhost:5173/giai-do.html (also Xưởng Nối menu Trợ giúp → "Chơi giải đố"). Decision record: `plans/reports/brainstorm-261101-1357-puzzle-game-report.md`; agent reports `plans/reports/fullstack-261101-14*-*.md`.
- **Runs on the real engine.** A level (`app/src/puzzle/types.ts` PuzzleLevel) = locked `base` graph (must contain a win-lose node; 3D levels lock a camera node) + `shownPorts` (locked node with `{}` = hidden scenery) + `tray` + `editable` params + `par` + `timeLimit` + reference `solution`. `run-level.ts` plays it headless (tests prove every solution wins and wrong answers don't). Node `pos` uses a logical 1000×560 board; cards stay readable, only positions scale.
- **8 levels** `app/src/puzzle/levels/level-01..08.ts` (one file each, `PUZZLE_LEVELS` in index.ts): 1 Bot đầu tiên, 2 Cửa kho, 3 Nhặt sao (2D ngang/từ trên) · 4 Hai nút một cửa (two robots take turns on buttons → teaches Hoặc) · 5 Đủ ba thùng, 6 Né lính gác, 7 Cầu nhấp nháy, 8 Xưởng tổng hợp (3D). Locked objects carry `params.name` (card subtitle).
- **UI** `app/src/puzzle/ui/**` + `app/giai-do.html` + `src/puzzle-main.ts` (Vite multi-page): level list with unlock + stars (localStorage `xuongnoi.puzzle.progress`), tap-port-then-tap-port wiring, real board cards + wire energy reused, tray, hint, editable param editor, CHẠY/DỪNG, win overlay. Stage: play look, overview fit before AND during the run (user asked 2026-11-01 17:45: zooming on the character looked bad); follow only if the player taps the toggle "Toàn cảnh / Theo nhân vật" (`GameView.setStageFit`). Dev fixture only via `?fixture`.
- **4 new character looks** (render/character-styles): robot-wheel (Chắn bùn mount), robot-legs, cat-chibi, cat-quad; in Nhân vật "Kiểu dáng". Cats are always black (`fixedColor`); robots tint with "Màu" (levels use #7cc4f0).
- **4 new sensors** (logic, now 54 kinds): speed-sensor, ahead-sensor, vision-sensor (new WorldApi `lineOfSight`, `world/line-of-sight.ts`), color-sensor (matches the block's colour against its Màu 1/2/3). Handbook pages + lesson "Giác quan cho bot" (`course-pack-e.ts`, end of Bot chapter).
- **Hộp input `hiddenWhile` "Ẩn khi bật"** (last input): level-follow hide/show, acts only on change; unwired = old behaviour.
- **Visual tutorial (16:30):** `puzzle/ui/guide.ts` + `guide-script.ts` + `guide.css`: Mèo Mun mascot (`mascot.ts` = engine cat-chibi in a round frame, talks: typing + mouth flap + head nod, natural blinks; face API `rig.face` in cat-chibi.ts, `blink.ts`), spotlight, tapping hand, dotted ghost wire; steps generated from `level.solution`; levels 1–7, auto only on first open (`xuongnoi.puzzle.guideSeen`), "↻ Hướng dẫn" replays; "?" = ghost of next missing wire (costs a star except while the guide leads). Approved preview: https://claude.ai/artifact/3zeovA1nDaLFRd9oY37cgR. In-game chibi blinks naturally. Hẹn giờ has "Tự lặp lại". Level 7 redone (timer repeat → latch toggle → go + Không → bridge Ẩn khi bật); a test now replays every level's solution through the real `canConnect` (self-wires were rejected by the board but not by runLevel). Level 7 can still be won without the bridge wire (bridge stays shown).
- **Hints & stars (17:30):** level 1 guide is mandatory on the first visit only (replay button on level 1 only); levels 2–8 have no auto guide — each "?" reveals ONE next step (cat + spotlight + hand + ghost wire). `freeHints` per level (L2 1, L3–5 2, L6–7 3, L8 4); `starsFor(level, answer, hintsUsed)`: 3 if pieces ≤ par else 2, minus 1 per hint beyond free, min 1. "?" badge shows free hints left (red + crossed star when none). New-piece intro: one cat line per unseen kind on first open (`intro:<id>` in guideSeen). Decoy tray pieces in levels 2, 3, 4, 7 (tests prove no cheaper shortcut). The task box hides while the cat talks. Repeat "?" on the same step counts (user may want to change).
- **Polish + APK track (from 17:50):** decision record plans/reports/brainstorm-261101-1753-cozy-polish-apk-report.md. Android toolchain installed: JDK 21 `D:\android\jdk-21`, SDK `D:\android\sdk` (platform 35, build-tools 35.0.0, platform-tools/adb 37.0.1; Google SDK license accepted with the user's approval), activate per shell with `D:\android\env.ps1` / `env.cmd`; user env ANDROID_HOME/ANDROID_SDK_ROOT set; JAVA_HOME and system PATH untouched (Java 8 at D:\Java stays). An older SDK (android-34, jdk17 zip, 2025-09-25) sits at the D:\android root — left alone, user may delete. Style previews A/B/C: https://claude.ai/artifact/Cp51RqEctmLeNhifT7fy8N , QTjjmT3rMqqb2YkzxxBduU , Gg4bamT5XnpHZaWavNuYCk (plans/ui-mockups/cozy-style/). **User chose A "Xưởng gỗ ấm áp" as default and wants B and C kept** (puzzle/ui/themes/, switcher A·B·C, localStorage xuongnoi.puzzle.themeTrial, DEFAULT_THEME=a). The floating A·B·C switcher overlaps the board corner at 844×390; move the choice into a Settings screen in part C.

## 2. Current state before 2026-11-01 afternoon (main was 2f223eb, 1496 tests)
- **Run:** `cd app; npm install; npm run dev` → http://localhost:5173 (strict port 5173).
- **Gates (in app/):** `npx vitest run --testTimeout 120000` (~4–7 min; slow when agents run), `npx tsc --noEmit -p .`, `npm run build`.
- **Stack:** Vite 8, TypeScript strict (noUncheckedIndexedAccess, noUnused*), three.js, vitest (node env, no DOM tests), plain DOM, no UI framework.
- **Engine:** typed ports bool/number/object, fixed 60 Hz tick, one world with 3 view modes (2d-side, 2d-top, 3d). `Runtime.takeRisen()` reports bool outputs that rose since the last read (used by the wire effect).
- **50 node kinds in 7 categories** (registry `app/src/nodes/index.ts`, count asserted in `tests/nodes/step-1-nodes.test.ts`):
  - input: key, stick, on-start, ui-button, ui-pick, ui-dialog, ui-start
  - logic: and, or, not, compare, touch, select-number, latch, zone, distance
  - math: const, math, counter, timer, random, map-range
  - object: character, block, item, vehicle, portal, moving-platform, rotating-platform
  - output: win-lose, show-number, show-text, guide-arrow, ui-meter
  - action: carry, interact, hand-tool, health, enter-exit, cargo, camera, teleport, shooter, spawner
  - brain: patrol, go-to, route-follow, chase, sequence, fight
- **Open params ("Nâng cao").** `ParamDef.advanced: true` folds a param under "Nâng cao ▸" in the properties panel. Defaults always equal the old constants, so old boards behave the same. Examples:
  - Every body (Hộp, Đồ vật, Nhân vật, Xe): collider size/offset (`world/collider.ts`) and gravity scale; Đồ vật also has friction and bounce.
  - Character hit reaction; carry drop distance and throw lift; fight backoff.
  - Vehicle brake, reverse, crash speed and shove; cargo gap and cells per layer; route arrive radius and steer gain.
  - Chase catch and flee distances, slow radius.
  - Sensors:
    - zone: rotate with follower, goods filter, rule edge/center/inside, min count
    - touch: grow, rule, hold time, "Vừa thôi chạm" output
- **Big features added this period:**
  - **UI nodes** (button, pick, meter, dialog, start screen).
  - **"Kho game của tôi":** a localStorage library (Tệp menu).
  - **Node groups:** Ctrl+G groups, Ctrl+Shift+G ungroups, double-click enters, Esc leaves, Ctrl+D duplicates; saved as `graph.groups`.
  - **Block styles:** 16 "Kiểu dáng", including Băng chuyền and Đường ray.
  - **Traffic:** route-follow, vehicle collision, guide arrows, cargo by goods type.
  - **Conveyor belts:** Hộp params plus "Chạy băng"/"Đảo chiều" inputs; riders stand on moving vehicles.
  - **Trains:** route-follow "Cách chạy: Bám ray" with 32 points; Đầu máy and Toa tàu models; "Kéo theo bởi" coupling (`world/vehicle-train.ts`).
- **16 samples** (`app/src/io/samples.ts`):
  - side-stars, top-collect, 3d-hop, key-door, pickaxe-rock, guard-patrol, cleaner-bot, maze-chase, platform-bot, arena, race, dice-zones, portal-camera, dodge-shoot
  - city-delivery (2D)
  - city-delivery-3d: 687 nodes, 32 groups, generated by `app/scripts/gen-city-delivery-3d.mjs`. Its `SIZES` table must cover `estimateNodeSize`.
- **Course: 30 lessons in 9 chapters.**
  - `app/src/tutorial/course.ts` holds the `COURSE` chapters. `arrangeCourse` orders `LESSONS` and renumbers the titles.
  - New lessons register through `course-pack-{a,b,c,d}.ts` (`createCoursePackX(kit)`).
  - The menu is grouped by chapter. Progress is stored by lesson id, so reordering keeps the ✓ marks.
  - `tests/tutorial/course-coverage.test.ts` fails if any node kind is not used by some lesson.
- **Sổ tay mảnh ghép** (Trợ giúp → Sổ tay mảnh ghép, or "?" on palette items and in the properties header):
  - `app/src/handbook/`: one page per node kind, with purpose, when to use, every port, every param, example, tips, lesson link and a "Thử ngay" tryBoard.
  - Levels Cấp 1–5 come from the lesson's chapter.
  - `tests/handbook/handbook-coverage.test.ts` fails if a node kind has no page.
- **Board look:**
  - Node cards use the "C · Khối đồ chơi" look (`editor/graph/node-cards.css`) at the OLD footprint (min 168 px, 26 px rows, inputs then outputs). Pins sit inside the card.
  - Wire "dòng điện" (`editor/graph/wire-energy.ts/.css`), toggled with Xem → Hiệu ứng dòng điện:
    - play: lit, dim, sparks
    - edit: stepped flowing dots
    - no CSS filters (they dropped FPS to 4)
  - `editor-state.loadBoard` runs `spreadOverlaps` (graph-geometry.ts), which pushes overlapping cards down per scope.
- **3D model pack "MEGA PACK WORLD"** (Mnostva Art, licensed Unity Asset Store pack the user owns):
  - Source `D:\asset\hieu333.unitypackage` → extracted + converted to coloured GLB in `D:\asset\hieu333_extracted\` (tools in `_tools\`, preview `MEGA_PACK_WORLD\Preview\index.html`).
  - All 2142 GLB copied to `app/public/models/mega-pack-world/` (570 MB, **gitignored**, not redistributable). Worktrees need a junction for this folder, same as node_modules. `npm run build` copies it into dist.
  - `render/pack-models.ts` (loader, ONE shared Lambert material, PackSlot, placements footprint/stand/inside/tile, tiled copies baked into one mesh) + `render/pack-model-table.ts` (style/vehicle/item → model pools).
  - Replaced by models (old code builders DELETED): block styles house, shop, stall, factory, tree, lamppost, trafficlight (+lamp overlay), road (orange), sidewalk, field, rock, fence; vehicles car, truck, bike; items crate, veggie, ore, bucket, ball, key.
  - Still code-drawn: box, sign, conveyor, rail, locomotive, wagon, all characters, tools (sword, pickaxe, hammer, torch, wand, lock, box item).
  - Variant per entity = `pickVariant(pool, ownerNodeId)` (items: ownerNodeId|entityId). Model colours are baked: "Màu" no longer tints replaced looks (handbook updated); traffic light colour still picks the lit lamp. Drivers are hidden inside car/truck models.
  - Mapping preview `plans/ui-mockups/model-mapping/index.html` (+ `build-mapping-preview.py`, `mapping.json`). Pools later trimmed: models > 20k triangles and the City_4/Farm_14 fences removed.
- **Approved preview artifacts:**
  - Node style + electricity: https://claude.ai/artifact/LK9fmQK5QZGmug49tPxZ9d (local copy `plans/ui-mockups/node-style-preview/index.html`)
  - Group demo: https://claude.ai/artifact/4U1nFMPX9o7pDvNSVVTqaf

- Artifact publishing: the built-in `Artifact` tool may be missing; use the `claude-artifacts` MCP `publish_files` (republish needs the UUID slug + base_version — get them from `list_artifacts`, the short URL id is not the slug). Multi-file pages (HTML + PNG) publish fine.
- Mascot source: `D:\asset\Black cat` = the user's own team mascot as a Live2D/VTube Studio model (ava.png, 2× 8192 atlases, 8 expressions: ANGRY, BIG EYE, BLUSH, box, CRY, logo, sleepy, suprise). Rebuilt in 3D cozy style; reference copies in `plans/ui-mockups/black-cat/ref/`. All 9 expressions + 4 motions exist in both cat pages.
- Screenshot scripts per page: `shot-*.mjs` next to each HTML (Playwright by absolute path).

## 4. Workflow that works (keep doing this)
1. **Requests.** The user writes short Vietnamese requests via `/brainstorm`.
   - Scout the code first, then reply in simple Vietnamese with tables: findings → options → ⭐ recommendation.
   - Ask plain-text numbered questions (`1a 2b`) and never popups. Unanswered questions take the ⭐ default; say so.
   - Visual decisions: build a quick artifact preview first (load the `artifact-design` skill, write HTML to the scratchpad, take one Playwright screenshot, publish with the `Artifact` tool), then implement after the user picks. To show it to the user, `Artifact action:open` or PowerShell `Start-Process <url>`.
2. **Decision record.** Write a short `plans/reports/brainstorm-<yymmdd-hhmm>-<slug>-report.md` before spawning agents. `plans/**` is gitignored, so reports stay local.
3. **Shared contract first, then parallel agents.** Before fanning out, commit the shared contract yourself so agents never edit the same file:
   - `ParamDef.advanced` + panel fold
   - `course.ts` + per-agent `course-pack-x.ts`
   - `handbook/types.ts` + per-agent `content-*.ts`
   - the agreed DOM hooks between agents (`data-live`, `is-hot`)
4. **Worktrees (manual):**
   - `git worktree add -b feat/x ../GameBuilder-x HEAD`.
   - Create the dependency junction YOURSELF with PowerShell: `New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path <wt>\app\node_modules -Target D:\workspace\GameBuilder\app\node_modules`. Some agents (fullstack-developer) have no PowerShell tool and get BLOCKED.
   - Give each agent a dev port (5201+), an explicit file-ownership list and the "do not touch" list of its siblings.
   - Agents use `subagent_type: fullstack-developer` with no `isolation` param (`isolation: worktree` fails here).
5. **Merge as each finishes:**
   - `git merge --no-edit feat/x`, then tsc + vitest (focused first, full at the end of a batch).
   - Clean up:
     1. Remove the junction with PowerShell `(Get-Item $j -Force)` after checking `LinkType -eq 'Junction'`, then `.Delete()`. Never a recursive delete through a junction.
     2. `git worktree remove --force ../GameBuilder-x; git branch -d feat/x; git worktree prune`.
6. **Verify yourself after merging:**
   - Run a combined Playwright check against :5173 (scripts in `plans/browser-scripts/`, e.g. `handbook-check.mjs`, `board-combined.mjs`).
   - LOOK at the screenshots.
   - Many real bugs were only caught this way: grey headers, overlapping cards, wrong search order.
7. **Wrap up.** Report to the user: what changed, how to open it, what was verified and what was NOT. Update this file.
8. **Design-preview loop (characters, mockups):**
   - One standalone HTML page per variant, written by one agent each (fullstack-developer, no worktree needed: different files in `plans/ui-mockups/<topic>/`). Give a shared style brief (palette hexes, face rules, "everything rounded", three.js pinned via importmap) so siblings match.
   - Agent must screenshot every state with its own `shot-*.mjs` and Read the PNGs. Then YOU look (contact sheet with PIL) before publishing.
   - Publish with the `Artifact` tool (same file path = same URL, new version). Also open locally with PowerShell `Start-Process <file>` when the user says "mở local".
   - Iterate by `SendMessage` to the SAME agent (it keeps the code in context) — much faster than a new agent. Paste the user's screenshot path; tell it exactly what to remove/move.
   - The user circles problems in red on screenshots (`c:\Users\trong\OneDrive\Pictures\Screenshots\...`). Read them; act on exactly what is circled.
   - For fine-tuning, add sliders + "Sao chép thông số"; when the user sends values, make them the defaults (and what "Đặt lại" restores).
9. **"Mở web local"** means the app at http://localhost:5173: start it in the background (`npm run dev -- --port 5173 --strictPort` in app/) if `curl` gets no 200, then `Start-Process http://localhost:5173`. Puzzle game: http://localhost:5173/giai-do.html. "Mở trên Chrome" = `Start-Process "C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe" <url...>` (several URLs = several tabs).
10. **Puzzle level workflow (proven 2026-11-01):**
   - Contract first (types.ts, run-level.ts, one stub file per level + index), then one agent per group of levels.
   - Every level needs headless tests: reference solution wins; empty answer fails; removing each solution wire/piece fails; tray counts; shown ports only; no key/stick; locked cards don't overlap in 1000×560; **every solution wire passes the real board `canConnect`** (runLevel alone bypasses it).
   - Then look at it in the browser: unlock levels with localStorage `xuongnoi.puzzle.progress` (Record<id,{stars}>) and `xuongnoi.puzzle.guideSeen` (array of ids + `intro:<id>`), open a level by clicking its card (a hash reload returns to the list), click pulsing ports with `{force:true}`.
   - Scripts: `plans/browser-scripts/puzzle-levels-check.mjs` (all 8 levels × desktop/phone), `puzzle-hint-overlap-check.mjs`, `puzzle-run-overview-check.mjs`, `puzzle-default-theme-check.mjs`.
11. **Compare looks on the REAL game, not only mockups.** When the user wants to "see the levels in each style", add a temporary switcher (`?theme=` + floating control + localStorage) on the real page and screenshot all levels per option + one contact sheet each. It caught issues mockups hid (flat floors clash, moon behind a door pillar).
12. **Agent briefs for UI work must list what is untouchable**: node cards, wires/wire energy, characters, level data. Ask for a pixel diff of one card per variant (it was 0/9480 for A/B/C).
13. **When the user answers only some questions**, apply ⭐ defaults for the rest and say which defaults were used; re-ask only the ones that block the next step (app name).
14. **(11-02) Loop that worked all day:** user request → I scout + restate in simple Vietnamese (ASCII sketch for anything visual) → user "ok/A" → append one line to §0z → `git worktree add -b <fix|feat>/<x> ../GameBuilder-<x> HEAD` + 2 junctions (node_modules, public/models) → one agent per independent area with its own dev port (52xx), explicit file ownership and "do not touch" list of the parallel agent → merge as each finishes → check `git diff HEAD~1 --stat` vs `--stat -w` (fix CRLF flips with a byte-safe node script, see scratchpad `to-crlf.mjs` pattern) → tsc + full vitest → LOOK at 1–2 shots myself → kill the agent's dev port, delete junctions, remove worktree, delete branch → §0z line → short Vietnamese report with "chưa kiểm" list.
15. **Restate before building.** The user's words are short and often mean something else than the first reading (see §5 "Reading the user 11-02"). For every visual/behaviour request, first reply with "Mình hiểu là…" + an ASCII before/after sketch and 2 options; build only after "ok". This saved several wrong builds (door edge-on, outline glow, Layout B swap).
16. **Physics/logic rule:** any change touching movement, carry, doors or levels must keep `tests/puzzle/physics-invariants.test.ts` green; new mechanics add an invariant first (red), then the fix (green). Never ship a level whose win can happen for the wrong reason.
17. **"Tắt/dừng agent"** from the user = pause while they are away (use TaskStop; worktrees keep the work). When they return and say "tiếp tục", resume the SAME agents with SendMessage, not new ones. It is not a permanent ban on agents.
18. **Previews:** agents cannot publish artifacts (no claude-artifacts tools in subagents) — they build the HTML, I publish with `mcp__claude-artifacts__publish_files` (republish = same slug + `base_version` from the last publish result) and open it with `Start-Process <url>`.
19. **Editor stability first.** Anything that also touches Xưởng Nối goes behind a per-game setting (default OFF for shipped boards) and must prove "OFF = byte/trajectory-identical" over all samples + lessons + handbook boards before merge.

20. **(11-03/04) Merge-conflict hygiene with many parallel agents:** agents branched from an old main conflict in shared files (nodes/index.ts, node-names.ts, step-1-nodes count, handbook content + counts, course.ts / course-pack-e.ts, guide-script.ts + guide/hint tests, solid-off-baseline.json / solid-on-summary.snap.json, city-delivery-3d.json). Do NOT resolve big conflicts yourself: `git merge --abort`, then SendMessage the SAME agent "merge latest main into your branch, keep-both, node count = N, regenerate only new snapshot entries, rerun full suite". If main moves again while it works, send the new hash. Node count after two parallel node additions must be bumped by hand (git auto-merges 55→56 twice into 56).
21. **Agent finished on a merged-with-main branch → fast-forward merge** (`git stash; git merge --no-edit feat/x; git stash pop` because HANDOFF.md is usually dirty), then the agent's own full-suite result is valid for the identical tree; still LOOK at 1–2 shots.
22. **Read-only audit agent before rule-wide changes** (e.g. "every action needs a wire"): general-purpose agent writes plans/reports/audit-*.md with VIOLATION/GREY/OK + file:line + fix + impact, verified by headless runs in the scratchpad. Translate to simple Vietnamese table, ask ⭐ options, then one engine agent (phase 1: engine + migration + minimal level wiring to stay green) and later a polish phase.
23. **Engine default changes must ship with a load-time migration** that adds VISIBLE wiring (not hidden defaults) and prove 110 old boards hash-identical (sample/lesson/handbook baseline). Keep migration in one module, run on every load path (parseBoard, loadBoard, lessons, handbook try-boards), always set the marker.
24. **Previews: hand-coded sims are only for look & rules.** When the user asks "is this really the engine?", answer honestly; the real level must be built on general nodes with anti-forced tests. Tell the user which parts are mockup.
25. **UI agents get an explicit style brief:** "match style A wood look; header buttons look like the card ×; no new toolbars/colours; cards/wires/characters unchanged". After merging any visible change, screenshot the real game and show the user before moving on.
26. **Pause = TaskStop** ("dừng dừng"); worktree kept; note state in §0z; resume the SAME agent with SendMessage.
27. **Small UI fixes the user circles: do them directly in main** (Edit + puzzle tests + rerun an existing Playwright script against :5173 with a copied `*-v2.mjs`), commit, show the shot. No agent needed.
28. **(11-04 night) Behaviour-vs-look split.** When the user approves a preview, they approve the BEHAVIOUR. The agent brief must say "behaviour only; preview chrome (board colour, header, zoom %, switches) is NOT ported; no CSS edits" and ask for a pixel compare of a card + tray chips at zoom 1 before vs after. Worked for free board (tray 0 px diff, CSS untouched).
29. **Preview from the previous preview.** Reuse the closest existing mockup folder (wire-tidy had real cards + L5/L7 boards) → copy into a new folder and adapt; add an "Cũ / Mới" switch so the user compares old vs new in one page. ~11 min agent run.
30. **Let the user try a branch before merging:** run the branch's own dev server on its agent port (`cd ../GameBuilder-x/app; npm run dev -- --port 52xx --strictPort` in background), `curl` it, `Start-Process http://localhost:52xx/giai-do.html`. Merge only after "ok".
31. **Infra requests ("kết nối VPS", "vào máy X"):** read the GLOBAL memory first (`C:\Users\trong\.claude\projects\*\memory\*vps*`) — project skills can be stale. Test reachability read-only (`ssh -o BatchMode=yes <alias> hostname`). Never print secret files (passphrase.txt, keys) — list names only. Never probe unknown hosts with several keys; ask the user for the exact user+key.
32. **Context near limit:** when the hook warns (~90%), write HANDOFF §00 + §0z line FIRST, then answer.
33. **Answer only what was asked.** A short question ("màn 6 con lính tự di chuyển à?") gets a fact-check from code + ASCII + table + options, not a rebuild.

34. **(11-04) Deploy = static release folders + symlink.** Build locally, stream ONE `tar | ssh vps40` into `/srv/xuong-noi/releases/<ts>`, `ln -sfn` current, then verify by comparing the live `assets/puzzle-*.js` name with the local build and loading both pages headless. Deploy only after the user explicitly says "deploy"; tell them to Ctrl+F5.
35. **New Caddy site on a shared box:**
    - back up the Caddyfile, append the block, check that the old bytes are unchanged;
    - `sudo -u caddy caddy validate` (validating as root breaks the log-file owner);
    - `systemctl reload caddy`;
    - curl the other sites.
    If the first ACME attempt ran before DNS existed, Caddy backs off: one more `reload` after DNS works fixes it in about 15 s.
36. **VPS access:** read global memory first; one `-o BatchMode=yes` attempt per host, alias only (`ssh vps40`, never the bare IP); never probe with several keys; a password prompt means STOP. For a ban, measure ports (22 vs 80/443) and tell the user their public IP for the boss.
37. **Cheat audit before trusting a level:** headless mutation runs, i.e. remove or move each story object, cut each hidden wire, and check whether it still wins. A read-only audit agent did all 7 levels in about 6 min. Run it whenever the user asks "sao nó biết…?"
38. **Behaviour-only fixes the user reports in their own words** (wires wobble, cards jump): reproduce with Playwright first (jitter tap, real drag, pan, drop overlap), measure (wires changed, px moved, frame ms), fix directly in main, re-measure the same script, then show one screenshot.
39. **When a subagent says "tsc clean", run tsc yourself after merging.** On 11-04 a TS2322 in the agent's own test file only showed on main (`npx tsc --noEmit -p .` includes tests/).
40. **Resume the SAME agent with SendMessage for iterations** (L3 v1→v2→v3, step v1→v2). Tell it to `git merge main` first when main moved, and to leave its dev port running for the user.
41. **The user tries branches on their own ports** (5234 L3, 5236 step). Open the exact URL in Chrome for them (`Start-Process chrome <url>`): they looked at :5173 and "didn't see" the feature.
42. **Previews of new text/UI:** take a REAL game screenshot and overlay only the new element in HTML (artifact via `publish_files`, same slug for revisions). Say clearly which part is mock. Look at your own shots before publishing; a CSS specificity bug (.bubble.strip beat .hidden) was caught that way.

43. **(11-16/17) Direction first, then pieces, then samples.** Order that worked: research + client feedback → user picks segment/first product → design the PIECE set (ports + open params table + ASCII wiring of what the learner builds) → user approves → agents build pieces in parallel → integration agent → sample agents. Never start from "a robot that follows a line"; start from the general pieces a learner wires.
44. **Gap report before building.** When the user asks "are the pieces enough?", grep the code for the exact dependency (e.g. does probe-ahead read the chassis heading?) and answer with a table: machine → enough / missing → proposed piece + size. Build only after the user approves the list. Sample agents get the rule "if a piece is insufficient STOP and report, do not fake it, do not touch the engine" — they then list palette gaps in their reports.
45. **Split by file ownership, name the siblings.** Pieces: one agent per pair of kinds, each with its own new files + a NEW course-pack file + its own handbook content file; shared registries (nodes/index.ts, node-names.ts, course.ts, handbook/index.ts, count tests, baselines, fake-world.ts) will conflict — tell every agent the expected final node count and "minimal, additive edits there".
46. **Integration agent instead of hand-merging three piece branches.** Branch from main (already holding the first branch), merge the other two keep-both, unify duplicated chapters, make the pieces work together (sensor follows chassis heading), build the first sample. For a later branch built on another unmerged branch (arm on mechanics): branch FROM that branch, then SendMessage the same agent "merge main into your branch, expected count N, single chapter, union baselines" and fast-forward main.
47. **Baselines after any merge:** script-compare against the pre-merge commit (`git show <base>:tests/editor/solid-off-baseline.json`): 0 changed / 0 missing except entries you can name. For `solid-on-summary.snap.json` regenerate with `vitest -u` on that one test, then run the same comparison (a reorder is fine, a changed row is not).
48. **Usage limit / pause:** agents die with a 429 when the 5-hour limit hits; their worktrees keep the work. When the limit resets (or the user says continue) resume the SAME agent ids with SendMessage ("check git status/log first, finish the remaining items"). "Dừng toàn bộ" = TaskStop every running agent, keep dev ports unless told otherwise; I cannot auto-resume after N minutes — say so.
49. **Load discipline with 5–6 agents:** every brief says "focused tests first, ONE full vitest at the end with --testTimeout 300000, rerun alone anything that only times out". Two tests always time out under load (tests/puzzle/level-7-decoy 900 s search, city-delivery-3d red light 180 s hard limit): treat as load, then prove with one full run on a quiet machine before deploy (3197 green on 11-17).
50. **Visible changes: preview → choose → port → look on the real app.** Arm look: standalone HTML with 4 looks + states + sliders (agent, ~10 min) → open the local file in Chrome (no need to publish) → user picks → SendMessage the SAME agent to port, render-only → I read shots → merge. Shell wording/buttons: build on a port, user says ok, then merge.
51. **One port for the user.** Many agent ports confuse a non-coder ("the step button is the old one" = wrong port). After merges run main on :5173, shoot every sample with one script (plans/browser-scripts/main-all-samples-check.mjs), then give a single link. When giving several ports, say what each one has and how to recognise the right one.
52. **Client-facing text for the user:** short, natural Vietnamese, only claims that exist in the product, plans phrased as "hướng tới"; give a table "claim → real state" so the user knows what is a plan. One link when the user says one link.
53. **(11-17/18) Wave shape that worked for a big physics change:** design report → spike that MEASURES the risky library (determinism across node / Chromium / vitest, bundle size, numbers against hand formulas) → numbered questions to the user → build behind a default-off world switch → on-mode hash baseline next to the off-mode one. Never start the risky half before the user answered.
54. **Contradicting answers:** when two picks cannot both hold ("own model" + "boxes must tip"), say so, state the reading you will take (own model for robots, library for crates only) and ask again in the next batch; build only the half that does not depend on it.
55. **Briefs name the sibling's files.** Two agents ran in parallel on wave 2 (crates / motor force) with a "do not edit motor.ts or robot-traction.ts" line in one brief and "stay inside motor.ts…" in the other → both merges clean. Still expect ONE cross-effect in shared baselines (see lesson "baseline recorded on a branch").
56. **After merging two branches, the full suite on main is the only real check.** Each agent's own full run was green; the merged tree had 1 red. Run it once on main, in the background, and do not tell the user "done" before it ends.
57. **A red hash test after a merge:** name the cause first (which merged change can move that board), re-run twice to see the SAME new hash, check the off-mode guard is untouched, re-record only the named entry, commit it alone with the reason in the message. Never `-u` the whole file.
58. **Agents that add a dependency:** npm inside a worktree replaces the junction with a real folder, so main does not get the package → after the merge run `npm install` in main's app/ before tsc. On cleanup check `(Get-Item path).LinkType` and unlink only real junctions; `git worktree remove` then deletes the agent's private copy.
59. **Use the waiting time.** While the user's answers are pending, run the "not verified" list (browser check of the last merged feature in the other view mode, lessons not yet played) instead of stopping; write each result into §A.
60. **Status questions ("xong chưa") get the number first:** green / red counts on the exact commit, then what changed, then what is still unverified, then the open choices. If a background run is not finished, say so and do not guess its result.
61. **(11-18/19) "The samples still run the old way" = check the switch, not the engine.** A feature behind a default-off world switch is invisible until shipped boards turn it on. After building such a feature, list which samples / lessons use it and turn it on there (pieces only), or tell the user plainly that nothing shows it yet.
62. **Sample overhaul brief:** two agents by sample family; "params / wires / cards only, NO edits under src/world, src/nodes, src/engine; if a board cannot work with pieces, STOP on it and report the missing rule or piece". Each stop found a real engine bug (pusher shoved the chassis under the crate; grip popped a resting crate 0.5 m). Then send the SAME agent back with permission to fix that one rule generally.
63. **A user screenshot beats green tests.** "Thùng dính vào tay máy" came after I had reported "35 placed, 0 dropped". Order: copy the screenshot into plans/reports, debugger agent → reproduce headless with per-tick metrics + browser frames from the same angle → name the frame the screenshot shows → root cause with numbers → failing test first → general fix → before/after frames. Then say to the user which check was missing (tests counted arrivals, not how the crate sat in the hand).
64. **Tests must measure what the eye sees.** For anything held, pushed or stacked add per-tick geometry assertions (overlap < 1 mm, centred < 1 cm, gap to faces) with an independent measuring helper (tests/helpers/grip-metrics.ts), not only end-state counts.
65. **Two baseline conventions will collide when siblings re-record.** Decide it in the brief: shipped boards saved with a switch on are hashed in the off-guard with the switch removed (plainRules) and in the on-guard as saved. I unified it by hand at merge time (one exported id set, on-guard widened to "loose items OR saved with the switch on").
66. **Full-suite reds while agents run are noise until proven otherwise, and must be proven.** city-delivery-3d (180 s hard limit, 120 s on-hash entry) failed four times under load and once "alone" with 21 node processes alive. Do not re-run while the machine is busy; wait until no agent runs, run the whole suite once, then report. It was green (565 s) on the quiet machine.
67. **UI redesign order that worked:** audit + ONE self-contained preview page with switchable directions (real labels, real card anatomy, light/dark, modes, tablet/phone, measured contrast), screenshots read by me → open the local file in Chrome → publish as an artifact only when the user asks to share → user picks a direction → port in steps on its own port → user looks → merge. Port step 1 = tokens + cards + wires in the old layout; step 2 = layout.
68. **Read the whole preview file before publishing it** (it was written by an agent): check for local paths, emails, external scripts; give it a short title; tell the user the page is private until he shares it from the Share menu.
69. **Options the user asks for mid-way go INTO the existing preview** (a "Độ sáng" toggle with 4 surface maps took 3 small edits + rebuild), plus a plain side-by-side sheet (do-sang.html, 4 screenshots, click to enlarge) because a non-coder may not find a new toggle. Republish the same artifact path so the link stays.
70. **"Keep X" in a restyle brief must say which part of X.** I wrote "keep the đường điện"; the agent kept today's wire drawing. The user meant: cards and wires fully in the new style, only the scene assets untouched. Write: keep the IDEA (current running along wires, three port type colours), restyle the drawing; and list the excluded folders (src/render, public/models, src/puzzle).
71. **Bigger cards break saved layouts.** Spread shipped boards at open time (positions only, hashes untouched, test over all 139 boards) instead of editing 140 board definitions; never move a user's saved board silently, offer "Sắp xếp gọn" once.
72. **"Gộp thành 1 bản local duy nhất"** = merge every finished branch into main, full suite on the merged commit (or fast-forward to a commit whose full run was that exact tree), stop the extra dev ports, remove worktrees, re-shoot on :5173, give one link. A branch based on an older main must merge main first (the agent does it and re-runs its checks).
73. **(11-19 → 11-23) Wave shape for a big feature:** phase 0 = previews + spikes with numbers and NO change to main (UX preview, asset preview, measuring spikes in a never-merged worktree) → user picks → wave of agents with disjoint folders (core / pieces / physics rules) → merge each as it lands → perf agent last on the merged tree → ONE full vitest on a quiet machine. Each wave ends with something the user opens on :5173.
74. **Merge order and the stash dance:** HANDOFF.md is always dirty → `git stash -q; git merge --no-edit <branch>; git stash pop -q`. If the merge CONFLICTS the pop is skipped and the stash is kept: resolve, `git add`, `git commit --no-edit`, THEN `git stash pop`. Check `git status` shows ` M HANDOFF.md` again before going on.
75. **Baseline files conflict when two branches each touch a named entry** (real-physics-on-baseline.json: one branch re-recorded `sample:agv-shuttle`, the other added `sample:sorting-factory`). Keep BOTH sides' intent by hand, then run both hash guards on main. Tell agents: add / re-record only NAMED entries, by hand, never `-u`, and list each in the report with the reason.
76. **Tell a running agent when main moves** (SendMessage: "main is now <hash>, merge it before your final run, report numbers from the merged state"). A branch that merged main and ran the full suite there can be fast-forwarded: its run IS the run on main.
77. **Agents cut off by the API (529 overloaded) or by the account limit (429 "weekly limit"):** not the agent's fault, work on disk survives. Same session: resume with SendMessage ("stopped by a server error, continue; run git status first"). After a session restart the old agent ids are NOT reachable (ListAgents is empty) → spawn a NEW agent with a full brief: worktree, head commit, which files are uncommitted and what each is (keep / debug-only / must be reverted), the failing tests with their messages. Put "commit OFTEN" in every brief. Do not hammer a 529: wait a few minutes (a background `sleep 300` notifies).
78. **Before re-briefing an inherited worktree, read its diff yourself** (`git diff --stat`, the test files). I found a debug edit in a shipped test that the brief had forbidden; the new brief named it and required `git checkout <base> -- <file>` before the final run.
79. **"Dừng agent" = TaskStop on each, write the paused state (worktree, branch, head, uncommitted files, junctions) into HANDOFF, restart nothing until the user says so.** He asked to continue two days later with "chia agent tiếp tục làm các việc dang dở".
80. **Build work goes to agents, the main session coordinates** (user, 11-22: "chia agent ra làm đừng làm trên phiên chính"; memory feedback-delegate-not-main-session). I had built two mock-ups myself over many silent turns. Main session keeps: worktrees + junctions, briefs, merges, reading shots, publishing artifacts, reports, small glue fixes.
81. **When the user asks to SEE the plan, give something that moves, made of our own figures.** Order that worked: status page with screenshots (he said "ý là cái xưởng nó hoạt động") → 2D canvas mock-up ("làm kiểu 3D dùng asset của chúng ta được không") → 3D page importing the game's real figure classes with a hand-written animation → several layouts on one page with buttons → the same in a warehouse → outbound flow. Label on the page, in Vietnamese: which figures are the game's, which are newly drawn, and that the motion is an illustration.
82. **Mock-up and game are two things; say so every time.** The mock-up lives in app/tests/showcase (dev-only page, never shipped), may fake everything, and its code never moves into src/. Each mock-up agent must deliver a list "What the real game would need" (thing → smallest GENERAL piece). The real sample stays pieces + wires + circuits with anti-forced tests.
83. **Publishing a 3D page as an artifact:** one HTML (no doctype / html / head / body; `<title>` first; CSS inline) + ONE script as a supporting file (`files: ["xuong-3d.js"]`, `root` = its folder). Pack the dev page with Vite's `build({ configFile: false, build: { lib: { entry, formats: ['iife'] } } })` from a script file (esbuild is NOT installed; Vite 8 = rolldown). No network at run time (code-drawn figures, system fonts). Update the SAME artifact by passing `url`. Always also write a local copy with a full HTML skeleton and open it (`start "" "<path>"`), and test THAT copy from file:/// with Playwright (page errors, a long headless trial, shots).
84. **A mock-up needs a headless trial hook:** `window.__mock.trial(id, seconds)` returning counts, the longest standstill, an overlap audit and a conservation check (in = in cages + in trucks + in transit). Run every layout 900 simulated seconds; "it looked fine for 20 s" hid nothing here only because this ran.
85. **Scenery the user approved "cùng style"** is drawn with src/render/factory-kit (rbox, cyl, bake / bakedMesh, VERTEX_TOON, VERTEX_GLOW, canvasTexture) and baked per layout, so it sits in one family with the game's factory looks. Keep it draw-only and separate from machine logic: "lớp máy" (pieces, proven) first, "lớp cảnh" (looks) on top.
86. **Restate a layout request with an ASCII before / after and say what it costs before drawing.** "Giống nhà máy Viettel / Amazon" = grid floor with drop holes in rows and columns, robots between them, stations on the edge — not a loop with chutes at the sides. Offer sizes (9 / 15 / 35 holes) with the risk of each; he picked the SMALLEST (A), not my ⭐ (B).
87. **A question the user leaves blank takes the ⭐ default only if I said so in that message and repeat which defaults were taken in the next one**; never for a change of behaviour of a shipped sample (the bare-deck drift: I asked `10a/b/c` and waited).
88. **End of a long day:** full vitest on the merged main on a quiet machine, remove every finished worktree (unlink the 2 junctions first), update §A-newest + workflow + lessons here, update memory project-xuong-noi, and list for the user what is waiting on him.

89. **A new session cannot reach old agents.** Agent ids die with the session; a stopped or crashed agent leaves only its worktree. Before relaunching: `git log` + `git status` + `git diff` in the worktree, write what each uncommitted file is into the new brief, and tell the new agent "find out how far the last one got". The inherited branch may hold RED committed tests (the paused sample had 4).
90. **Briefs that worked on 11-23 / 24 always carried:** worktree + branch + head, the two junctions ("never delete or reinstall"), its own dev port and the ports that belong to others, an OWNS list and a do-not-touch list, the gate in numbers, "report gaps, do not force them", "if a hook blocks a command, report it; do not get around it", the report path, and the Status block. A brief containing a "get around the hook" line is refused by the permission classifier.
91. **Vitest on a loaded machine: always `--testTimeout 300000`.** With the default 5 s, five heavy files fail on main too (tests/arm/side-tray-robot-goto, tray-robot-goto, tests/puzzle/level-5-board-pieces, physics-invariants, tests/traffic/grid-anti-forced). Two tests have their OWN limits and still time out under load (city-delivery-3d-sample 180 s, level-7-decoy 900 s): rerun them alone, never call them green unseen.
92. **Check a doubtful failure on main before blaming the branch:** run the same files in the main repo and in the worktree, same flags. On 11-23 this showed 15 failures on main vs 17 on the branch at 5 s, and 253 / 253 on both with the long timeout.
93. **Gate for every engine rule:** measure first; hash guards off = 0 moved, on ≤ 2 named entries, no visible outcome of a shipped sample changed; re-record by hand, name old → new and the physical reason; a test that goes red without the rule; no sample name, card id or kind check in the condition. Five rules passed this way in one day with 2 entries moved in total.
94. **Merge rule:** fast-forward when the agent's full run was on a tree that contains the current main; otherwise `git merge --no-ff`, then the controller runs tsc + the off / on / invariants guards + the touched tests on the merged tree and writes that number down. HANDOFF.md being dirty does not block a merge unless the branch touches it.
95. **When main moves under a running agent, tell it at once** (SendMessage: new head, which files changed, which baseline rows were added or re-recorded, "merge main before your final run, keep both sides' named rows, never `-u`"). A sample agent had to merge three times in one evening; that is the price of parallel waves and it should be said to the user.
96. **Agents may be forbidden to write report .md files.** Three of them returned the whole report as their final message. The controller then writes the numbers into a dated HANDOFF block the same turn; otherwise they are gone at the next compaction.
97. **Look previews before porting:** one static page per group with lettered options the user answers in one line (`L-b D-a N-a`, `X-a T-c C-a S-b K-c 6a 7a`); the agent states a recommendation and what is rough; the controller looks at the shots before sending the link. Then ONE looks agent ports every pick as opt-in draw-only styles with a byte-identical proof on old boards.
98. **Serving a preview page:** the controller's own attempts to start `python -m http.server` were denied by the permission classifier twice; the user started it. Give the command in the shell the user's `!` prefix runs (bash: forward slashes, `cd /d/workspace/GameBuilder && python -m http.server 5371`), and give the URL that matches the folder the server really started in. `Start-Process <url>` from the PowerShell tool opens the user's browser.
99. **Hook words:** the scout hook blocks Bash commands that contain `node_modules`, `dist`, `.git` or `build` as a path. Do not rephrase to slip past it. What works without touching those words: `npm run build`, `git` subcommands, `curl`, and the proven deploy line run from the PowerShell tool as §00 documents it.
100. **Head start across waves works when files are disjoint:** measuring steps and preview pages (no source change), lessons (tutorial/ only), looks (render/ only), a world rule (src/world only, one owner at a time) ran beside the sample agent. What must wait: anything that edits the sample files, and a piece whose look or world call is still being built.
101. **Measure before building a piece that moves things.** The cage spike showed a cage of blocks falls apart when moved (40 of 48 runs) and that 154 cartons miss the tick budget, before a line of the piece existed. The hitch agent measured the dock seam and found a cage does not ride a moving platform. Each finding went to the user as a question with a tested recipe beside it.
102. **Deploy sequence used on 11-24 (adds safety to the §00 recipe):** `npm run build` → `npx vite preview --port 5180` + headless check of both pages → read-only `ssh vps40` check (current release, releases kept, disk) → upload to a NEW release folder WITHOUT switching → compare bundle names on the server with the local build → `ln -sfn` → headless check of the live site, including opening the newest sample from the menu and running it → write the rollback line into HANDOFF and tell the user.
103. **Tools for the live check** (session scratchpad, rebuild if gone): `npm install playwright-core@1.49.1` in the scratchpad, `chromium.launch({ channel: 'chrome', args: ['--use-gl=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader'] })`; one script lists console errors and failed requests of `/` and `/giai-do.html`, another clicks Tệp → Bài mẫu → the sample → Chạy and reads the HUD text. The only console error on both old and new live is the missing favicon.
104. **Pushing the project somewhere is a decision round first:** check `git remote -v`, what is ignored and how big it is, licensed assets, third-party dumps, secrets; then ask where and what. On 11-24 there was no remote at all.

## 5. Lessons learned, bugs and fixes
- **Agent hand-off claims can be wrong; check before acting.** Example: "Quay lại sửa highlights the wrong button" was false, because `controller.playAware` already swaps play→stop while playing.
- **The new card CSS overrode the category colour.**
  - Cause: `.wc .wc-node { --cc: grey }` loaded after `.wc .cat-* { --cc }` at the same specificity.
  - Fix: put the default on low-specificity `.wc-node`.
- **The bigger cards broke every saved layout.**
  - The first C-style cards were wider and taller than `estimateNodeSize`, so old boards overlapped.
  - Fix: keep the old footprint, keep `estimateNodeSize` = old formula, and add `spreadOverlaps` on load.
  - Lesson: any card-size change must keep saved boards readable.
- **Adding inputs to a node grows its card.** Hộp got "Chạy băng"/"Đảo chiều", so sample layouts overlapped and the city sample overlap tests failed. Fix: respace samples (the 3D one by regenerating with matching `SIZES`); `spreadOverlaps` now also covers user boards.
- **Wire effects and FPS.** CSS `filter: blur/drop-shadow` on SVG wires took the city board from 60 to 4 FPS, and a continuous dash animation did the same. Use plain wide translucent strokes and `steps()` animations.
- **One-tick pulses.** Pulses are invisible if the UI samples every 100 ms, so `Runtime.takeRisen()` records rising edges between reads.
- **Search ranking.** Grouping search results by section hid the best match. While searching, show one flat list in score order.
- **Sensor "chạm mép" is too loose for delivery pads.** Hence the rule options (center inside / fully inside) and hold time.
- **Delivery dropped goods on the road.** Cargo unload only places items beside the truck. The discussed fix is "Giao vào", which is parked.
- **Tutorial:**
  - Never assert lesson positions or numbered titles in tests; use `lessonName()` and `chapterOf()`.
  - LESSONS items are copies made by `arrangeCourse`, so compare by id, not identity.
  - Step checks match by kind + port, never by id or position.
  - A node cannot wire to itself; timer loops need an extra Hoặc.
- **Merge conflicts:**
  - The usual spots are `nodes/index.ts`, the `types.ts` WorldApi tail, `fake-world.ts` stubs, `world.ts` step order, and the `vehicle.ts`/`character.ts` params + `createOnly`.
  - Resolve by keeping both sides.
  - `pathfind` must stay the LAST param where present (a test checks it).
- **Line endings.**
  - Many files are CRLF or mixed. Edit with a Python script that detects `\r\n` and writes bytes back.
  - `sed -i` on CRLF files is fine for single-line substitutions.
  - Check that `git diff --stat` stays small.
- **Tooling quirks:**
  - The Bash hook blocks commands containing the words "node_modules" or "dist".
  - Bash heredocs that contain Python with `'\r\n'` or nested quotes sometimes fail with "unexpected EOF". Write the script with the Write tool and run it.
  - PowerShell has no `git` on PATH; run git from Bash.
  - The dev server must be started in the background (`npm run dev -- --port 5173 --strictPort`).
- **Stray worktree folders.** A stray vitest run from an agent can keep a worktree folder locked ("Permission denied"). Wait for the agent's "finished" notification, then remove the folder after checking it contains no junctions.
- **Tests.** Full vitest takes 4–7 min while agents run, so always pass `--testTimeout 120000`; city sample sims are slow. vitest has a node env only, so DOM behaviour is checked with Playwright from `plans/browser-scripts` (Playwright is imported by absolute path from an old scratchpad `.../091c5a2d-.../scratchpad/smoke/`; it is not in package.json).
- **Known engine limits:**
  - head-on route cars deadlock
  - houses always face +Z
  - the start screen does not pause the sim
  - Spawner has no goods tag
  - zone goods match is exact
  - wagons fold back on ping-pong routes
  - no character step-up

- **Worktree cleanup order (slipped once 2026-11-01):** delete the junctions FIRST, then `git worktree remove`. Git stopped at the junction that time and nothing was lost, but don't rely on it. Also kill leftover agent dev servers (`netstat -ano | grep :52xx`).
- **Agents flip line endings.** Twice on 2026-11-01 a merge rewrote whole CRLF files to LF (game-view.ts, app-contracts.ts; and my own `sed` on level-05.ts). After every merge compare `git diff HEAD~1 --stat` with `--stat -w`; if they differ, convert back byte-safely with node (`replace(/\r?\n/g,'\r\n')`). Git Bash `sed`/pipes strip `\r`.
- **Puzzle levels:** block "Ẩn đi" is a one-shot pulse — use "Ẩn khi bật" for doors that follow a signal. Prove every level headless (solution wins, each wire removed fails) before looking at the UI.
- **Puzzle bugs found and fixed on 2026-11-01:**
  - *Level 7 unplayable*: its solution wired the timer's "Hết giờ" into its own "Đếm lại"; the board forbids self-wires but `runLevel` didn't care, so tests passed while players and the guide got stuck. Fix: Hẹn giờ "Tự lặp lại" param + level redesign + a test replaying every solution through `canConnect`.
  - *Level 4 AND bypass*: one sensor straight to the door also won. Fix (user picked 4b): both robots must pass, each holds the door for the other → the level teaches Hoặc.
  - *3★ was automatic*: trays held exactly the solution pieces. Fix: decoy pieces (L2 extra touch, L3 extra Hoặc, L4 Và, L7 Và). A decoy "Không" was rejected: an unwired Không outputs true and opened the door with 1 piece.
  - *Stage looked like the editor* (grid, tiny scene) → play look + fit the scene to the visible stage; characters tiny → follow toggle; the user then disliked auto-zoom on run → overview by default.
  - *Cards unreadable on phones* (whole card scaled to the 1000×560 fit) → scale positions only, cards ~1.0 desktop / ~0.78 phone, spread overlaps.
  - *Mascot bubble overlapped the task box* → `.pz-screen.mascot-on .pz-task { visibility:hidden }`.
  - *Mascot cat too small in its frame* → tighter portrait framing (FRAME_SHARE 0.9).
  - *Number editor closed after each +/−* (made "set the number" hint steps unusable) → fixed in board-view.ts.
- **Reading the user (2026-11-01):** "thô" on the wheel robot meant the body sat bare on the wheel (wanted a connector/decoration), not faceted geometry — ask or show both when a word is ambiguous. "Lấy y chang" = copy the look AND the effects (wire energy). "Biểu cảm mới" after the talking preview = put talking/blinking on the cat itself. "Thay giao diện" = never the characters, cards or wires.
- **My own slips today:** removed a worktree before its junctions (nothing lost; order now in §4); left an agent dev server on :5207 (kill with Stop-Process); `node -e` with nested quotes/backticks broke twice — use the Edit tool or a script file; a `sed` on a CRLF level file flipped endings — use node byte-safe edits; inline Playwright imports in Bash are blocked by the hook (path contains "node_modules") — write the script to a file first.
- **Preview-page pitfalls:** a guide variable named `step` clashed with the simulation's `step()`; `page.setContent` can't load `file://` images (navigate to the image instead); pulsing ports are "not stable" for Playwright clicks (`force:true`).

- **Pack model lessons:**
  - FBX from Unity: texture path points at the author's PC; vertex colours must be dropped (Unity ignores them, glTF multiplies them → a black pizzeria); units are cm (scale 0.01); mergeVertices shrinks GLB ~3×.
  - Give all GLBs one shared material/texture, otherwise every model uploads its own copy of the palette.
  - Triangles, not draw calls, were the cost: measure with `renderer.info` (temporarily expose it) before optimising. Square/corner models tile into dozens of copies — keep tile pools to long thin pieces.
  - `sed -i` on a CRLF file rewrote all line endings (game-view.ts): restore with `git checkout -- file` and use the Python byte-safe edit.

- **Character art: do only what the user asked.** Self-added details were rejected every time on 2026-11-01: cheek puffs, round fur clumps ("popcorn"), pointed fur spikes, then shell fur (soft but 5–8× triangles: 40–66k vs 8k). All reverted to plain smooth shapes. Offer extras only as an optional toggle or a question.
- **Jagged face edge** (robot legs): the cream face was a second sphere intersecting the head → noisy seam. Fix: a separate flattened ellipsoid sitting just in FRONT of the head surface; never coplanar/intersecting shapes.
- **Collar iterations (cat-quad):** a ring "computed to hug body+head" became an oversized float tube around the back; a short-neck tiny collar hid under the head; the final answer was a plain circular torus at the neck + user sliders + the X pendant placed each frame at the ring's lowest-front point hanging straight down (otherwise the chin hides it).
- **Tail rendered pale** (chibi): inside-out geometry winding. Fix the winding, don't paint over it.
- **Shell fur pitfalls (if ever retried):** a square-grid strand mask shows a dotted grid and dark specks on white; use jittered cells + a rotated second octave, keep the lowest ~30% of layers solid, very light root darkening on white.
- **Tooling:** the `claude-artifacts` MCP server can be down; the built-in `Artifact` tool still publishes. Preview pages need internet (three.js + Google Fonts from CDN). Agents' dev servers/Playwright can keep worktree folders locked; stop them (`Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort ...` → `Stop-Process`) before deleting.

### Lessons, bugs and fixes — 2026-11-23 → 11-24 (newest)
- **Cartons crept backwards on the robot tray at every start (57 tips counted for 58 cartons).** 0 → 1.5 m/s in under 0.25 s moved a carton 4–6 cm per start, 359 mm of a 370 mm tray on long routes; one fell off and landed in the right box by luck. Fix in the sample's data: both motors "Độ tăng tốc" 400 → 250 rpm/s (slip 84 mm; 100 locks the floor). A count that is off by one is a physical event to trace, not a test to loosen.
- **Decorative wires hide in a working sample.** Cutting each wire one at a time found 9 wires and 2 cards that changed nothing (the "Đang chờ" outputs of "Bảng giữ ô", a "tray flat" pair). They were removed. A fault-only wire must be cut on a board where the fault happens, or the cut proves nothing.
- **A cage of five plain blocks came apart when moved (40 of 48 runs).** Cause: a carton leaning 0.06 mm on a side wall counted as "pushed ahead" by that wall, was placed past the wall's end inside the front wall, judged squeezed, and the step was refused. Fix: slide rule (reach ≤ 10 mm across the way is not a push) + `world.moveBlocks` for bodies of several blocks. Side effect found by the tick-by-tick compare: invisible gripper collision cubes used to lag 1–4 ticks; 2 baseline rows moved for that reason only.
- **No palette piece could move a cage.** Found by testing three candidates instead of assuming one would work. The piece shipped honestly as "stands still until a hitch exists"; the hitch followed.
- **A cage set down on a moving platform hangs in the air when the platform leaves.** Moving blocks carry characters, items, vehicles and robots, never blocks; cage legs are draw-only. Recipe that works: a second hitch whose carrier is the platform, one per slot.
- **A moved body passes through fixed walls; a held one does not.** The hitch rule stops the held body flush and puts the carrier back by what the body missed. Without it a carried cage would cross walls and other cages.
- **Dead start at 1.9 m/s pushed a carton corner 29 mm into a 50 mm cage wall** when the test moved the cage itself; with a real chassis as carrier the worst was 1.96 mm in the plate and 0 in the wall, because the chassis' own acceleration (2–3 m/s² loaded) is the ramp. Measure with the real mover before adding a limit.
- **The stations' real carton (0.32 × 0.26 × 0.28) does not fit a 0.8 m cage six times** (7 of 8 runs, pile to 1.13 m); the 0.26 m cube used in the first spike did. Measure with the object the sample really makes. 1.0 m also solved the tug fit, one decision instead of two.
- **Lesson text said "xanh dương" for a robot drawn orange.** The board set the default blue; the renderer deliberately draws that default as the look's orange (render/robot-models.ts, OLD_DEFAULT_COLOR → LOOK_COLOR). Seen by the controller in a shot. Fix the words, and check the same phrase in handbook notes (one more was found there).
- **An agent's "83 failures" were all timeouts of a loaded machine**; a first reading as "regressions" would have blocked a correct merge. See workflow 91–92.
- **The sample's own real-physics hash row moved (4fcec266 → ee671a3a) when the slide rule came in, with identical run results.** Cause not isolated. Open item: find which entity differs.
- **My `!` command for the user used backslashes** (`cd D:\workspace\GameBuilder`); his `!` runs bash, the `cd` failed, the server started in `plans/` and my URL gave 404. I said so and gave the right URL. Use forward slashes in any command meant for `!`.
- **Vite re-optimised dependencies in the shared node_modules junction** when an agent started a second dev server; restart :5173 if it misbehaves afterwards.
- **Report "full suite" precisely:** which commit, which flags, how loaded the machine was, exact counts including skipped and expected-fail. A partial check on the merged tree (guards + touched tests) is not a full suite and must be called what it is.

### Reading the user — 2026-11-23 → 11-24
- **"tạm dừng agent" = pause only** (stop the tasks, keep the worktrees, write heads and uncommitted files down). **"làm tiếp các agent còn dang dở" = check first, then resume the same work**, not new work.
- **"các đợt nào có cái nào làm được làm luôn"** = he wants the waves in parallel so no single step feels heavy; give a table "running now / cannot start yet and why".
- **He asks "sắp xong chưa" every few minutes while something long runs.** Answer with what was looked at just now (time, last commit, uncommitted files, what the log shows) and an honest estimate marked as a guess; say when no percentage can be read. Do not repeat a long status.
- **He picks against ⭐ often on looks** (X-a, S-b, K-c, D-a): record the pick, state the cost once, build it, never substitute.
- **"hai cái hỏng đó nhỏ, để sau"** released the two timeouts only; it did not release the rest of the running suite. I kept waiting, and deployed when he then said "tạm dừng agent và deploy lên đi". Deploy needs his word; a mention of deploy inside a question is not it, an instruction is.
- **"có cái gì đang xài code ép không"** wants a checked answer: grep the engine for sample names, list the named stand-ins separately from forced code, and say which older samples were not re-audited today.
- **"nhà máy lớn"** is ambiguous (the full two-level design vs a bigger grid): asked N1, not answered yet.
- **"thu toàn bộ folder lẻ vào 1 folder chung để commit và push cho sếp, không thiếu bất cứ thứ gì"**: the folders are branches of one repo, there is no remote, and "nothing missing" collides with licensed models, a third-party dump and 746 MB of shots. Restate, list what cannot or should not go, ask P1–P4 before any push.
- **Explanations he asked for** ("logic, engine nào để xe đưa lồng vào xe") want a short table: step → piece → exists or not, then the engine rules, then the one unsolved point said plainly.

### Lessons, bugs and fixes — 2026-11-19 → 11-23
- **Value badges covered port names inside a running copy (found by me in an agent's shot; the agent had read 9 shots and missed it).** Cause: a wire's value badge was always drawn at the wire's middle, even over a card. Fix: editor/graph/badge-placement.ts slides it along the wire to the nearest clear spot; no spot = no badge. A browser check now fails when a pill or badge box overlaps a port label. Lesson: read at least the "busy" shot of every UI agent yourself, at 100 % zoom.
- **Cartons counted twice in the chute (3 red tests).** "Dịch chuyển" put every counted carton on one fixed spot; it avoids solid blocks, not other cartons, so they stacked into a 2.9 m column that fell back into the zone. The tests were right, the circuit was wrong. Fix with pieces: counted cartons go onto a `belt-frame` out belt (17 in a line). Root gap: no piece removes an object.
- **A crate on a bare robot deck drifted outward 0.096 × offset per radian** once the chassis box turned. Three workarounds each broke something else. Real cause (measured in a bare engine scene): the rigid-body engine keeps the earlier contact of two bodies that do not move against each other, and a crate co-turning with the chassis is such a pair. Fix: refresh the chassis contacts in a turning tick + put a non-sliding rider back on the deck's arc + show a turning chassis along its arc. Result ≤ 0.35 mm over 9.5 rad, still slides at low friction. Lesson: when a constant error ignores friction, mass, speed and step size, it is geometry or stale state, not friction — measure in the smallest scene.
- **The tilt tray hung at 35° when the robot stood askew**: the chassis collided as an upright square that did not turn. Fix: the turned outline (above). Squaring up to 90° or tipping 60° were the pieces-only stopgaps.
- **Robots passed through each other; a tray's own parts were weighed as its cargo (70 kg false payload); a link had no width.** Found by the spike BEFORE building, fixed as general rules in wave 1.
- **Wrapping cards into a circuit can change the run order** (independent cards run in id order; inside a copy the id becomes `<copy>/<id>`). Every number of every entity stays equal, listing order may differ → the "behaviour unchanged" proof uses an order-free entity hash; the editor picks the copy id to keep the order when it can, else creates and warns. True cure would be in engine/compile.ts.
- **Wires drawn as dots after reopening a saved board** and **a new palette card colliding with a shipped note id `n1`**: both older than the circuit work, both found by the editor agents, both fixed (new ids skip notes, groups and copies).
- **"Hiện hết cổng mã" made a 12-machine dispatcher card 41 rows.** I had flipped a default; reverted and redesigned: one always-on input "Mã việc" + one output "Mã vừa giao", per-machine outputs stay advanced. Lesson: count the rows at the maximum before changing a default, and tell the user when the result differs from the option he picked literally.
- **Pack parcel models keep their own strong colours**, so a class colour is only a sticker → use the code-drawn carton (tape = class colour) wherever colour means something.
- **`belt-frame` walls were grey 0.3 m slabs hiding the cartons** → preview page with 4 looks, user picked B (low lip); done render-only: a wall is recognised as a plain block sharing the belt's ownerNodeId (`beltLipView`), the real wall keeps its height.
- **Mock-up: the hand-over stop was missed by a hair** (robot overshot the goal by < 2 cm and went round the whole loop) → "a hair past the goal counts as standing on it". Any stop-at-a-point animation needs a tolerance on the far side too.
- **Mock-up layouts on a one-way grid:** hole rows and hole columns must be ODD counts so the outer ring is one consistent circuit (row 0 east, col 0 north); otherwise a corner is unreachable.
- **scene-view.ts is the only CRLF file**: an agent's edit rewrote it as LF and produced a whole-file merge conflict. Edits with the Edit tool on single lines keep CRLF; say "keep CRLF" in briefs that touch it.
- **A `New-Item -ItemType Junction` into a worktree that did not exist yet created stray folders** (PowerShell could not find `git` in that call, the junction lines still ran). Order: `git worktree add` in Bash first, check the folder, THEN the junctions in PowerShell.
- **Bash heredocs with quotes failed twice** ("unexpected EOF while looking for matching '") even with a quoted delimiter. Write the script with the Write tool into the scratchpad and run the file. Same for any command that must contain the hook's blocked words (`node_modules`, `build`): put the path in a script, build it with path.join.
- **Playwright for the controller:** a script in the session scratchpad that imports `scratchpad/pw/node_modules/playwright-core/index.mjs` via pathToFileURL; chromium with `--use-angle=swiftshader --enable-unsafe-swiftshader --ignore-gpu-blocklist --enable-webgl`. The dev harness for real figures is tests/showcase/scene-harness.html (`window.__harness.showcase(...)`).
- **A file:// page gets no CORS header from Vite for pack GLBs** → serve previews that load models with `python -m http.server <port>`; packed mock-ups avoid models altogether.
- **The scratchpad of an earlier session id is still readable in a resumed session** (same absolute path), but background shells (dev server, http.server) are gone: check ports with curl and restart :5173 first.
- **An agent tried to pass a blocked path through an encoded string**; the permission system refused, correctly. Briefs now say: do not encode paths to get past hooks, use a script file.
- **Agents' own full-suite runs under load show the two known timeouts** (puzzle/level-7-decoy, city-delivery-3d) and sometimes need `--testTimeout 300000`; on the quiet machine the controller's run was 4338 / 4338 with the default suite settings plus that timeout.

- "máy chủ quá tải mà máy chủ nào … VPS à?" → explain plainly which server (Anthropic's), that his VPS and his PC are fine, and that nothing is lost.

### Lessons, bugs and fixes — 2026-11-18 → 11-19
- **Pusher shoved the chassis instead of the crate (AGV could not unload with the switch on).** contactSet counted anything overlapping > 1e-6 as pushed; a rigid crate rests 0.3 mm inside the chassis top. Fix: a body that only holds a rigid crate up is never pushed, and a pusher never pushes the body it is mounted on (linear-actuator.ts `holdsUp`, `pushBodiesOf(world, except)`).
- **Gripping a crate that rests on a table popped it 0.5 m up.** findFreeSpot read the resting contact as an overlap and lifted by LIFT_STEP. Fix: rigid crates are tested with 1 cm off every face (REST_SKIN). Also fixed for portals.
- **Crate "stuck" to the side of the hand (user screenshot).** Three causes: `betweenFingers` tested the grasp point against the crate's shadow per axis (a 0.6 m crate under a hand tilted 20° counted as 0.77 m wide); the grab froze whatever pose it had; the taught pose put the palm 15 cm inside the crate. Fix: world/grip-fit.ts (between the open fingers, grasp point inside the real box, palm / link not sunk in, face within 10° of the pads, each finger closes to its face) + boards retaught.
- **Non-solid fingers passed through crates.** Fix: world/hand-solid.ts, same push path as links; later exact oriented shapes (a tilted hand was up to 27.6 mm "fat" with upright cover cubes, now within 1 mm).
- **Released crate dragged by still-closed pads** (auto-arm, once per cycle) → the board waits 0.5 s on a timer before swinging home (7 steps). After a slip the pads now open instead of wedging the dropped crate.
- **A crate counted as held from the tick closing started.** Fix: held only when both pads are within 0.1 mm of their stop; pads stop 0.4 mm short of the face (landing exactly on it shoved the crate up to 5 cm).
- **Fast joint launched a crate** (120°/s: 17 m/s, 1346 J too much). Fix: joints give at most a = (available − needed)/m like pistons. A step slowed only by this rule must not light "Quá tải".
- **Share weighing made the sorter miss heavy crates** (reading climbs while the crate rides on; the push came 0.4 s late). Board fix with 4 cards: start the wait when a crate first loads the scale. With real crates 1.8 N of spare piston force is too slow: test "works again" at 1.5 × need.
- **One-wheel robot loops back to the start under real physics**, so "ended near the start" no longer proves a square: the anti-forced check also needs every side > 1.7 m.
- **A wireMaterial spawner with its mass input unwired makes a 1 kg crate** (found building the endless auto-arm): wire a constant.
- **Default "solid" for links broke a switch-off lesson** → third value "Tự động" (solid only when the board plays real physics) + legacyDefault for saved boards. Pattern for any default that depends on a world switch.
- **Agents add load-dependent assertions** (ms per tick with 8 workers): keep cost numbers in the report, not in the suite.
- **Agents force-add plans/ files with `git add -f`** although plans/ is ignored (reports, shots, a .json board ended up in commits). Harmless locally; say "do not add ignored files" in briefs if the repo ever gets a remote.
- **UI: card outline at ~2:1 in the preview** → 3.26 in the app; with the softer "Xám dịu" board it dropped to 2.86 and the faintest text to 4.37 → `--card-line` #767164, `--text3` #515b69. Re-measure contrast after every surface change; a test now forbids pure-white light surfaces.
- **UI: legend pill clipped its last word** because it was as wide as its box, not its words; found by reading my own shot, not by the agent.
- **UI: 60 % fit zoom made card text unreadable** → fit floor 0.85 (Học sinh) / 0.92 (Giáo viên); a board bigger than the window is panned.
- **Wires share code with the puzzle** → the new wire look is scoped to the editor (`.app` + `{ rich: true }`); puzzle pixel compare stays at 0 differing pixels. Check shared modules before "do not touch X".
- **Tool quirks (new):** a 100 KB preview source needs 3 Read pages (cap ~25k tokens per read) before it may be published; `Start-Process chrome <file or url>` opens things for the user; Stop-Process on the owner of a listening port stops a leftover agent dev server (`Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort N`); the browser scripts of an agent keep its worktree paths and port: after a merge run them with `BASE=http://localhost:5173` and fix the OUT folder; the scratchpad folder can vanish and come back mid-session, so keep anything worth keeping under plans/.

- "làm lại toàn bộ giao diện … chuyên nghiệp hiện đại … phù hợp giáo dục … giữ đường điện … màu trang khác" → wants samples to look at first ("làm vài bản mẫu cho tôi xem thử"), picks fast (`1c,2a,3a`, "chốt bản C", "xám dịu đi bạn"), and asks for an artifact to send to other people.
